## Account

### createAccountSetting

`account.createAccountSetting`

Gorgias REST: Create a setting.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.gorgias.api.account.createAccountSetting({"body":{"type":"x"}});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `body` | `object` | Yes | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="body full type">

```ts
{
  data?: Record<string, JsonValue>,
  type: string
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output:** `object`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts
{
  id?: number | null,
  data?: {
    send_survey_for_chat?: boolean | null,
    send_survey_for_email?: boolean | null,
```

### getAccount

`account.getAccount`

Gorgias REST: Retrieve your account.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.gorgias.api.account.getAccount({});
```

**Input:** _empty object_


**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `created_datetime` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `deactivated_datetime` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `domain` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `settings` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `status` | `Record<string, JsonValue> \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="settings full type">

```ts
{
  id?: number | null,
  data?: Record<string, JsonValue> | null,
  type?: string | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### listAccountSettings

`account.listAccountSettings`

Gorgias REST: List settings.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.gorgias.api.account.listAccountSettings({});
```

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `query` | `object` | No | — |

<Accordion title="query full type">

```ts
{
  type?: string
}
```

</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### updateAccountSetting

`account.updateAccountSetting`

Gorgias REST: Update a setting.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.gorgias.api.account.updateAccountSetting({"path":{"id":1},"body":{"type":"x"}});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `body` | `object` | Yes | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="path full type">

```ts
{
  id: number
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="body full type">

```ts
{
  data?: Record<string, JsonValue>,
  type: string
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

## Customers

### createCustomer

`customers.createCustomer`

Gorgias REST: Create a customer.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.gorgias.api.customers.createCustomer({"body":{"channels":[]}});
```

### deleteCustomer

`customers.deleteCustomer`

Gorgias REST: Delete a customer.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.gorgias.api.customers.deleteCustomer({"path":{"id":1}});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `object` | Yes | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="path full type">

```ts
{
  id: number
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output:** `void`

### mergeCustomers

`customers.mergeCustomers`

Gorgias REST: Merge two customers.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.gorgias.api.customers.mergeCustomers({"query":{"source_id":1,"target_id":1},"body":{"channels":[],"email":"agent@acme.test","external_id":"x","language":"aa","name":"x","timezone":"Africa/Abidjan"}});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `query` | `object` | No | — |
| `body` | `object` | Yes | — |

Merges and deletes the source customer; this action cannot be undone.

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="query full type">

```ts
{
  source_id: number,
  target_id: number
}
```
</Accordion>

### updateCustomer

`customers.updateCustomer`

Gorgias REST: Update a customer.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.gorgias.api.customers.updateCustomer({"path":{"id":1},"body":{"channels":[],"email":"agent@acme.test","external_id":"x","language":"aa","name":"x","timezone":"Africa/Abidjan"}});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `body` | `object` | Yes | — |

```ts
}
```

### updateCustomerCustomFieldValue

`customers.updateCustomerCustomFieldValue`

Gorgias REST: Update customer field value.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.gorgias.api.customers.updateCustomerCustomFieldValue({"path":{"customer_id":1,"id":1},"body":"x"});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `body` | `string \| number \| boolean` | Yes | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="path full type">

```ts
{
  customer_id: number,
  id: number
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `field` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `value` | `Record<string, JsonValue> \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="field full type">

```ts

  id?: number | null,
  external_id?: string | null,
  object_type?: Ticket | Customer | string | null,
  label?: string | null,
  description?: string | null,
  priority?: number | null,
  required?: boolean | null,
  managed_type?: customer_type | null,
  definition?: {
    data_type: text,
    input_settings?: {
      input_type: input,
      placeholder?: string | null
    } | {
      input_type: dropdown,
      choices?: string[] | null,
      default?: string | null
    } | null
  } | {
    data_type: number,
    input_settings?: {
      input_type: input_number,
      placeholder?: string | null,
      min?: number | string | null,
      max?: number | string | null
    } | null
  } | {
    data_type: boolean,
    input_settings?: {
      input_type: dropdown,
      choices?: boolean[] | null,
      default?: boolean | null
    } | null
  } | null,
  created_datetime?: string | null,
  updated_datetime?: string | null,
  deactivated_datetime?: string | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

## Files

### downloadFile

`files.downloadFile`

Gorgias REST: Download a file.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.gorgias.api.files.downloadFile({"path":{"file_type":"attachment","domain_hash":"Hna8kldnmlal","resource_name":"file-resource.png"}});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `object` | Yes | — |

Returns { kind: "binary", contentType, base64, sizeBytes }. The signed redirect URL is followed internally and is never returned or logged. Downloads above the client safety limit of 20 MiB are rejected.

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="path full type">

```ts
{
  file_type: string,
  domain_hash: string,
  resource_name: string
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `kind` | `binary` | Yes | — |
| `contentType` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `base64` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `sizeBytes` | `number \| null` | No | — |

### uploadFiles

**Risk:** `write`

## Ticket Messages

### createTicketMessage

`ticketMessages.createTicketMessage`

Gorgias REST: Create a message.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.gorgias.api.ticketMessages.createTicketMessage({"path":{"ticket_id":1},"body":{"channel":"aircall","from_agent":true}});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `query` | `object` | No | — |
| `headers` | `object` | No | — |
| `body` | `object` | Yes | — |

This operation can send a customer-visible message and is marked destructive. Set query.action and the documented If-Unmodified-Since header when using concurrency control.

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="path full type">

```ts
{
  ticket_id: number
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="query full type">

```ts
{
  action?: force | retry | cancel
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="headers full type">

```ts
{
  If-Unmodified-Since?: string
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="body full type">


## Tickets

### createTicket

`tickets.createTicket`

Gorgias REST: Create a ticket. Messages without sent_datetime are sent to the customer by Gorgias.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.gorgias.api.tickets.createTicket({"body":{"messages":[{"channel":"aircall","from_agent":true}]}});
```

`tickets.createTicketTags`

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.gorgias.api.tickets.createTicketTags({"path":{"ticket_id":1},"body":{"names":[],"ids":[]}});
```

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `body` | `object` | Yes | — |

```ts
  ticket_id: number
}
```

```ts
  ids?: number[]
}
```

### listTickets

### updateTicketCustomField

`tickets.updateTicketCustomField`

Gorgias REST: Update ticket field value.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.gorgias.api.tickets.updateTicketCustomField({"path":{"ticket_id":1,"id":1},"body":"x"});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `body` | `string \| number \| boolean` | Yes | — |

```ts
  id: number
}
```

### updateTicketCustomFields

`tickets.updateTicketCustomFields`

Gorgias REST: Replace all custom field values on a ticket. Fields left out of the request are deleted.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.gorgias.api.tickets.updateTicketCustomFields({"path":{"ticket_id":1},"body":[]});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `body` | `object[]` | Yes | — |

This route replaces all custom-field values on the ticket. Include every value you want to retain.

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="path full type">

```ts
{
  ticket_id: number
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="body full type">

```ts
{
  id: number,
  value: string | boolean | number | number
}[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output:** `object[]`

<AccordionGroup>

```ts
    object_type?: Account | AccountSetting | Customer | CustomField | CustomFieldCondition | CustomUserAvailabilityStatus | CustomerExternalData | Integration | Macro | Order | Role | Rule | SatisfactionSurvey | Shopper | ShopperAddress | Subscription | Tag | Team | TicketMessage | Ticket | User | UserSetting | View | Widget | string | null,
    label?: string | null,
    description?: string | null,
    priority?: number | null,
    required?: boolean | null,
    requirement_type?: visible | required | conditional | string | null,
    managed_type?: contact_reason | product | resolution | ai_intent | ai_outcome | ai_sales | ai_discount | ai_journey | managed_sentiment | call_status | string | null,
    definition?: {
      data_type: number,
      input_settings?: {
        input_type: input_number,
        placeholder?: string | null,
        min?: number | string | null,
        max?: number | string | null
      } | null

      data_type: text,
      input_settings?: {
        input_type: input,
        placeholder?: string | null
      } | {
        input_type: dropdown,
        choices?: string[] | null,
```
